                                                         12.0 Allies
                                        “I thought it was the end for us and the hive. Luckily we had some help.”
                                                                                                   - Karlon Feht, after the Scouring of Hive Orinocanna
Here the Game Master can find an assortment of Imperial NPCs, to be used as he sees fit, as allies, rivals, or even enemies. This chapter is only a
beginning, and GMs are encouraged to create new and unique NPCs for their games. GMs should feel free to modify the NPCs presented here, or use
them as a starting point for their own creatures and characters. The equipment and weapons listed in an NPC’s profile are typical for such individuals,
but certainly not exclusive; NPCs can carry whatever equipment the GM deems appropriate for the situation and the needs of his game.

                                                                       12.0 Allies - 919
                               12.1 Reinforcement Characters
                               "His loyalty couldn't be bought at any price; but it could be rented remarkably cheaply."
                                                                           - Inquisitor Allendyne after the execution of Rogue Trader Parnis Vermode

The player characters can often find themselves facing opponents far beyond their capabilities, either due to terrible accident, diabolical ambush,
or fiendish machinations. Much of the time, the characters might struggle simply to survive the encounter or root out a heresy. If they are prepared
though, and have established connections, they might be able to call upon outside aid in their war for the fate of Mankind.
          The Reinforcement system allows players not only to call upon mighty personages to aid them in such dire circumstances, but also gives
an opportunity for a player to become one of these powerful individuals for a session. These give players a sampling of play at higher levels, and
can offer a fun narrative scene with the altered group dynamics. These new characters also provide opportunities to play individuals who might be
unworkable for long-term roleplaying, but are ideal for singular sessions.
          It is important to note that these Reinforcement Characters (abbreviated as RCs) should be used sparingly and not part of normal ga-
meplay. The GM should ensure his players do not use this system as a crutch against ineffective investigations and poor preparations for their foes.
Such over-reliance might, for example, draw the attention of their master who could depress their Influence or express his displeasure in their
service through other, more painful, means.

                                                       12.1 Reinforcement Characters - 922
                                           12.2 Imperial Citizenry
Humanity across the galaxy numbers at least in the trillions, by the estimates made by the Adeptus Administratum, but that may be a conservative
guess, informed only by census-takers and flawed survey methods. The true numbers of Mankind among the stars is uncountable, and the diversity
therein doubly so. Even aside from the different strains of Abhumans, the populations of two planets can be so different as to be incomperable in
mindset, culture, nature of society, and so on. No two planets within the vastness of the galaxy are the same, even if they should largely fall under
the same descriptors, and even for those from the same planet there is no doubt that territorial divisions can cause great rifts in culture for peoples
from the same world. The only uniting factor for all of this disparity is one thing: that all are subjects of the God-Emperor of Mankind.
Life within the Imperium is a very different affair for nearly every person                                           Gangers
under the rule of the God Emperor. From the dense, unending urban              Gangs are violent, territorial groups that can be found throughout the
squalor of hive cities to the isolated loneliness of frontier worlds, the      Imperium. Hive Worlds are known for their extensive amount of gangs.
worlds of Mankind have an endless variety, united by the uncertain vaga-       Even Terra has a huge number of gangs formed from the destitute
ries of Warp travel and faith in the God Emperor. Even those who occupy        non-adept population. Hive world gangs are often part of the powerful
the same world are unlikely to share much in terms of life experiences,        rival families or clans of the hives. Powerful lower level gangs are some-
as how could a nobilite from the apex of a hive city possibly understand       times legitimized by the planetary government as the hive world's plan-
the perspective of a dreg in the underhive, or even that of a manufactora      etary defense forces, in order to keep down the most anarchic elements,
labourer in the mid-hive?                                                      and even to defend the planet if it is attacked.

                                                            12.2 Imperial Citizenry - 927
                                             12.3 Imperial Nobility
The Imperium is a realm that is beyond massive, in breadth of span and in it's scale of population. Such is the scale of population that there are
uncountable thousands of noble dynasties, with attendant numbers of houses therein, and thus accounting for millions of individuals of noble birth.
The nature of such a family's wealth is no constant among these multitudes, as the ranks of Imperial nobility can range from manufactora moguls to
Imperial Knight pilots to Navigators to Rogue Traders and every intermediary inbetween. Regardless of the source of a family's finances they are pos-
sessed of a lifestyle and accomodations that a more average civilian could scarcely dream of, with the more respectable of their ilk being possessed of
offices that are such great responsibilities as to warrant such rewards. But of course every family is apt to bear its share of scions ill at ease with work,
no matter how administrative or sedentary that work may be.

                    Imperial Houses                                                                   Imperial Nobilite (Troop)
Highborn exist on nearly all Imperial worlds, from the towering spires of                     Follower Type:                   Reinforcement Character Level:
its hive cities to the stone forts and caves of its feral kingdoms. They are                  Standard Human                              Majoris
the privileged and powerful of their worlds, those fit for governance not
                                                                                     Influence Cost:            Influence Minimum:           Other Requirements:
by the will of the people but by the providence of their birthright. While                  5                            45                     Peer (Nobility)
their powers and purview might differ from world to world, their func-
                                                                                               Influence: 35                             Profit Factor: 40
tion remains the same: to lead their people and control the resources and
political might of their planet. Most highborn grow up being groomed for          WS          BS      St         T        Ag       Int       Per      Wp          Fel
this power, either taught to govern justly by fair and evenhanded peers or,        25         25      20         20       25       25          25         20         40
more likely, instilled with a disregard for those below and ingratitude for
                                                                                      Movement: 2/4/6/12               Wounds: 6          Fatigue: 4
the influence and position they have been given. Many are so ingrained
                                                                               Skills: Charm +10, Command +10, Commerce +10, Deceive +10, Inqui-
into their opulent lifestyle that they have little or no notion of how the
                                                                               ry +10, Linguistics (Low Gothic, High Gothic), Scrutiny +10
majority lives. Generations can be spent in supreme—if relative—com-
                                                                               Talents: Air of Authority (1), Eloquent, Excessive Wealth (PF 40),
fort, be that a heated cavern shielded from perpetual blizzards or an orbi-
                                                                               Friends in High Places, Peer (Nobillity), Persuasive Charm, Rival (No-
ting pleasure-satellite that rides auroral clouds. Here they are content to
                                                                               bility)
shield themselves in the trappings of wealth and privilege, while focusing
                                                                               Traits: Size (4)
on the real threat to their existence: other highborn.
                                                                               Gear: -
           The scale and size of the Imperium is reflected in the nobility
                                                                               Armour: Best Robes (+10 to Charm)
of its worlds. Just as it has endured for millennia and covers the majority
of the galaxy, so too are there noble families, sector lords, and planetary
governors whose lineage stretches back thousands of years, and whose                                       Man-at-Arms (Troop)
holdings comprise whole systems or wide regions of space. It is also                          Follower Type:                   Reinforcement Character Level:
reflected in its diversity, with each world’s ruler as unique as the world                    Standard Human                              Minoris
itself. Some worlds are ruled through agencies such as the Adeptus
                                                                                     Influence Cost:            Influence Minimum:           Other Requirements:
Ministorum or Adeptus Mechanicus, where the rulers are more the result                      3                            35                     Peer (Nobility)
of power plays than hereditary bloodlines or the decree of the Adeptus
Administratum. Some rulers flicker and fade, having barely made their             WS          BS      St         T        Ag       Int       Per      Wp          Fel
presence known. Others form dynasties lasting the entire history of a              35         35      35         35       30       25          30         30         20
world. Often a family or lineage can draw great power to itself, slowly              Movement: 3/6/9/18               Wounds: 9            Fatigue: 6
but surely acquiring planetary control and resources, seeding its progeny      Skills: Athletics +10, Awareness +10, Discipline +10, Fortitude +10,
throughout positions of influence and authority until there is no place on     Intimidate +10, Medicae +10, Operate (Surface, Aeronautica) +0, Parry
a world untouched by its grasp.                                                +10, Scrutiny +0
           Being a highborn means more than being born into power and          Talents: Aspire to Vengeance, Bodyguard, Combat Formation, Iron Jaw
position; it means the lifelong obligations and struggles both to protect      Traits: Size (4)
the interests of the family or clan, and to try and better them. It is the     Gear: Medi-Kit (Advanced), Micro-Bead
nature of power that those that do not have it crave it, and those that have   Armour: Heavy Carapace (AP6, All)
it crave more. Noble families thus conduct warfare with each other to          Weapons: Hellgun -or- Boltgun, Chainsword
garner and defend power, in battles often masked with subtlety, disguise,
and innuendo, but no less deadly than open combat. At such levels, mere                                                 Hellgun
currency is worthless, and power is traded in favours and debts. In these          Class           Basic        Range          100m        Rate of Fire        S/3/5

                                                                    12.3 Imperial Nobility - 931
                                   12.4 Adeptus Administratum
The Adeptus Administratum is the administrative and bureaucratic division of the Adeptus Terra, consisting of untold billions of clerks, scribes and
administrative staff constantly working to manage the Imperium at every level, from assembling war fleets to levying taxes. It is the largest of the
departments comprising the Adeptus Terra - the mere administration of the Imperial Palace is estimated to involve over ten billion adepts.

Billions of Administratum officials on Terra and throughout the galaxy         The lower ranks of (and majority of personnel within) the Administratum
are constantly carrying out population censuses, working out tithes,           are filled with scribes of varying functions, minor census-takers, record-
recording and archiving information, and the million other things that are     ers, data-compilers, and so forth.
necessary for the running of the Imperium. Such is its immense size and
complex bureaucracy that whole Administratum departments have been
                                                                                               Administratum Scribe (Troop)
lost and forgotten. So dogmatically bureaucratic, whole departments of                    Follower Type:               Reinforcement Character Level:
adepts continue to diligently but purposelessly carry out the role their                   Lesser Human                           Minoris
department was founded for, even after the department has become long               Influence Cost:        Influence Minimum:      Other Requirements:
obsolete. Of all the Imperium's divisions, the Administratum is regarded                   4                        20                      -
as the least prestigious, but its high ranking members are among the most        WS      BS       St       T      Ag      Int      Per      Wp     Fel
powerful men in the Imperium. Despite its massive inefficiency, the Ad-
                                                                                  10      20      20       20     25       35       30       30    30
ministratum is so integral to the running of the Imperium, that without it,
the whole Imperium would grind to a halt. As a result, the Master of the             Movement: 2/4/6/12             Wounds: 6          Fatigue: 5
Administratum is the most powerful member of the High Lords of Terra.          Skills: Commerce +0, Linguistics (Low Gothic, High Gothic), Logic +0,
Many Administratum positions are hereditary; parents pass on their ranks       Scholastic Lore (Bureaucracy) +10
and positions to their children, who continue the work that had occupied       Talents: Archivator
the lives of their ancestors. Typical Administratum adepts labour within       Cybernetics: Calculus Logi Upgrade -or- Memorance Implants
gargantuan vaults, sitting at logic-engines and can be considered learned      Gear: Auto-Quill, Dataslate
by Imperial standards.                                                         Armour: Robes with Devotional Iconography (Administratum)
            Despite holding sway over untold trillions and managing a
galactic bureaucracy, the Administratum's primary method of record                             Administratum Master (Elite)
keeping and distribution of information is vellum. Parchment not only
                                                                                          Follower Type:               Reinforcement Character Level:
appeals to the dogmatic values and traditionalism of the Administratum,
                                                                                          Standard Human                          Majoris
but is also considered a far more secure and cheaper a method of data
collection than electronic counterparts such as dataslates or cogitators            Influence Cost:        Influence Minimum:      Other Requirements:
while being far more durable than standard paper. Enormous amounts of                      6                        40             Peer (Administratum)
vellum is produced from the hides of porcine bred on agri-worlds or ar-                    Influence: 40                        Profit Factor: -
tificially grown in bio-vats. Vellum is a prized resource in the Imperium,       WS      BS       St       T      Ag      Int      Per      Wp     Fel
so much so that inter-agency wars have been fought over it and wealthy
                                                                                  10      20      20       20     25       50       35       35    30
familial dynasties solely dedicated to the creation of parchment exist.
Legions of Administratum scribes work tirelessly, consuming untold                   Movement: 2/4/6/12              Wounds: 6            Fatigue: 5
amounts of the parchment in their endless recording of data.                   Skills: Command +10, Commerce +10, Linguistics (Low Gothic, High
            The ultimate fact of the Administratum's existence and massive     Gothic), Logic +20, Scholastic Lore (Bureaucracy) +30
span is that the agency is likely to have nearly any fact that exists in       Talents: Archivator, Mastery (Scholastic Lore [Bureaucracy], Logic),
recorded history somewhere in its archives. The most obvious barrier to        Talented (Scholastic [Bureaucracy], Logic), Unparalleled Proficiency
this being useful to those who would benefit from it is the organization       Cybernetics: Calculus Logi Upgrade, Memorance Implant, Scribe-Tines
of the Administratum itself, along with the sheer mass of information          Gear: Auto-Quill, Cogitator (Personal), Data-Loom, Dataslate
that they have in their possession. Finding a single shipping manifest         Armour: Robes with Devotional Iconography (Administratum)
that is older than a day may be an impossible task for any but the most
utterly knowledgable of scribes, and even then such a task will take time,
perhaps weeks or months of cross-reference, searching, and relearning
forgotten indexing methods to even be able to continue searching. This
is, of course, if such information is even to be found. As obsessive as Im-
perial bureaucracy is on recording things it is the sad state of things that
the archives of the Administratum can often lie forgotten, unmaintained,
or simply abandoned due to disasters, invasions, or the simple encroach-
ment of time.
            If, however, the monstrous depths of information can be
plumbed well enough by a knowledgable scholar then all manner of
things can come to be known.

            Eversor Combat Drugs - The Eversor has autorig injectors
filled with potent combat drugs designed to boost his performance. These
injectors use minute amounts of extremely potent drugs (meaning he
does not run out of drugs unless cut off from supplies for months). If he
is ever killed, his body explodes from the biotoxins erupting from his
metabolism, inflicting 2d10+5 Energy Damage with the Blast (5) and
Toxic (Fast, -30, Lethal) Qualities. At the beginning of each of his Turns,
he may choose one of the following effects:
            • Hyper-metabolics: The Eversor can perform one additional
Full Action (or two additional Half Actions) this turn. He can still only
perform one action with the Attack subtype this turn.
            • Berserker-Slaught: The Eversor gains the Brutal Charge (3)
trait until the end of his turn. Until the end of his turn, whenever he uses
the Charge action, he inflicts 1 additional hit on a target in range per
degree of success after the first on the Weapon Skill test.
            • Auto-coagulants: He makes a Challenging (+0) Toughness
test. If he succeeds, he immediately removes 4 damage.
            • Shock-stims: The Eversor ignores all penalties from Fatigue,
Critical Effects, or being Heavily Damaged until the end of his Turn.
Weapons: Executor Bolt Pistol, Neuro Gauntlet, Power Sword, Melta

                                                        12.4 Adeptus Administratum - 937
                                               12.5 Adeptus Arbites
The Adeptus Arbites are the police force of the Adeptus Terra, devoted to enforcing Imperial law throughout the entire Imperium. Utterly dedicated
and without mercy, the Arbites are feared throughout the galaxy, for they are the agents of a harsh law, where failure and incompetence are crimes,
and the only punishment is death. Arbites are empowered to act as judge, jury and executioner – citizens have no rights, and only members of the
Adeptus Terra or the Inquisition could claim anything so elaborate as a trial.

The Adeptus Arbites faces a monumental task in enforcing the law across                            The Ranks of The Arbites
countless Imperial worlds. Those that take the oaths, don the carapace ar-      The constant need for judgement and prosecution among the Imperium's
mour, and wield the shock maul are often obsessive and pitiless individ-        populace is met by the Adeptus Arbites, and to pursue this rightful fulfill-
uals, men and women who see the law as an extension of the Emperor’s            ment of Lex Imperialis they must be many in skills and numbers. While
will upon His subjects, and its enforcement their sacred duty. Whether          the ranks of any given Precinct-Fortress are well out-numbered by those
they are skilled in combat or possessed of a keen mind, Arbitrators know        in their jurisdiction it is a simple fact that even the lowest ranking Arbi-
they face an implacable and remorseless foe, one which they must count-         trator is worth at least three criminals. But, to aid in this skewed balance
er with all the talents at their command. To be part of this agency is to       the Arbites employ many specialists, with fields varying from infiltration
be apart from their original surroundings, for after training in the Schola     to the art of assassination.
Progenium, Arbitrators are never assigned to the world of their birth.                     Arbitrator: Arbitrators are the most commonly encountered
They are beyond local authorities, serving none but higher Imperial law.        members of the Arbites, they are their public face and those most often
Once a man puts on the distinctive dark armour he stops being merely a          seen persecuting criminals. The Arbitrators are the boots on the ground

cases, Arbitrators can face more danger from a supposedly loyal ally than       themselves with more weighty issues such as bringing the Emperor's
the scum they pursue.                                                           justice to rebels, hunting down enemies of the state, and purging heretics.

obligation, and local custom. Thus, they can vary from world to world or        execute both Planetary rulers and Adepts if they are shown to be commit-

pronouncing his own laws. Arbitrators care little for such trivialities, and               Chastener: Part interrogator and part chaplain, the Chasteners

inconsequential unless they affect the Imperium directly, are perpetrated       Judges are charged with breaking transgressors and extricating confes-
against Imperial officials, or somehow fundamentally threaten a world’s         sions along with doing the same to Arbites found to be possessed of

bastion of Imperial rule during revolutions or invasions, holding out to                   Investigator: Though many would not suspect such behaviour

                                                                                           Moritat: Ill-regarded and considered something of a neces-
                                                                                sary evil, Moritats are the embodiment of the "executioner" part of the
                                                                                Arbite's role as judge, jury, and executioner. Given special training once
                                                                                inducted into this order, and often kept separate from other Arbites, these
                                                                                Judges are licenced to kill any seen engaged in unlawful conduct, even
                                                                                other members of the Adeptus Arbites. This sanction is a heavy burden,
                                                                                and those who bear do not ever take it lightly, and thus they keep away
                                                                                from other members of the Arbites, so that their judgement, should such
                                                                                a time come, will remain untainted by personal feelings.

                                                                  12.5 Adeptus Arbites - 942
                                              12.6 Adeptus Astartes
When the Emperor set out to re-unite the lost tribes of Mankind at the very dawn of the Age of Imperium, he knew that he could only do so with a
mighty corps of warriors at his side who were as strong, skilled, and dedicated as he. To this end, he created twenty perfect beings in his own ima-
ge—the Primarchs—and from the genetic legacy of each of these he created an entire legion of superhuman warriors.
           The Space Marines of the Adeptus Astartes are the inheritors of the original Primarchs, whose blood still flows in their veins. Their bodies
enhanced by implants that boost their abilities far beyond the human and their hearts steeled by unmatched courage and devotion, it is truly said of
the Space Marines that they know no fear. They are the Angels of Death, bearing the most formidable of weapons and wearing the strongest of ar-
mours. Such blessings come at a cost however, for the Space Marines are recruited at a young age and know nothing other than war before eventually
and inevitably falling in battle against the endless foes of the Imperium. They are at once far more than human, yet removed from Mankind, their
entire existence one of sacrifice, duty, and death.
           Chapters of the Adeptus Astartes operate apart from the bulk of the Imperium’s power structure. Each is led by a Chapter Master who is
considered a peer of the Imperium, an equal to the highest-ranked servants of the Adeptus Terra. They may be petitioned for aid against invasion or
insurrection, or to spearhead the many wars and crusades launched against the foes of Mankind, but they are a power unto themselves and may very
well reject such a plea with no reason given as to why. Though it is said there are only a scant million or so Space Marines, the speed with which they
attack—and the devastation they cause—makes them rightly feared across the galaxy.

                                                                        12.6 Adeptus Astartes - 946
                                12.7 Adeptus Astra Telepathica
To ensure the Imperium’s safety, the Adeptus Astra Telepathica oversees the recruitment of these dangerous individuals. Its fleet of Black Ships travel
endlessly from world to world, gathering anyone who shows the taint of psychic talent to take back to Terra for testing. Only a fraction of those taken
survive to serve in the Adeptus Astra Telepathica. Most are sacrificed to sustain the Emperor, perhaps the most potent psyker even known. Others fall
during their training, their minds or bodies torn asunder by the creatures of the Warp, or executed should their powers prove too uncontrollable or
their minds unsuitable. Only after years of rigorous schooling in the Scholastia Psykana, the Adeptus Astra Telepathica’s training facilities, are they
deemed fit to serve the Imperium either among its armies or as part of the great network of Astropaths conveying messages across the stars.
The psykers and untouchables of the Astra Telepathica are very largely                               Sanctioned Psyker (Troop)
opposites (with psykers often shunning the presence of nulls unless such
                                                                                              Follower Type:                    Reinforcement Character Level:
a thing is utterly necessary), but they are both incredibly important tools                   Standard Human                               Minoris
of the Adeptus.

talented and verified to have a great measure of power of their psionic
abilities. While the least of such psykers largely serve as astropathic           WS          BS      St         T      Ag         Int     Per      Wp          Fel

such as biomancy to great effect on the battlefield, though mostly in a

small squad of peers. Primaris psykers, however, are much more singular

                                        12.8 Adeptus Mechanicus
With progress a thing of the dim and distant past in the 41st Millennium, what relics of the superior science Mankind once possessed that remain
are guarded jealously by the Priesthood of Mars—the Tech-Priests of the Adeptus Mechanicus. The vast majority of the technological wonders that
propelled Mankind’s very first wave of expansion into every corner of the galaxy are all but lost, and those that remain are understood by a vanis-
hingly small number. Technological understanding has long ago given way to superstition, and learning to ritual. Much technology has been lost in
Mankind’s incessant wars for survival, strewn across the million worlds of the Imperium and the ruins of a million more. Advancement is regarded
with supreme distrust and innovation is all but unknown, leaving each world with its own unique mix of surviving technology. Some worlds har-
bour ancient atmospheric processors that filter out poisonous elements, but the populace employs no tool more advanced that a flint-tipped arrow.
On others, advanced weaponry is commonplace, but must be used to hunt local wildlife or else the population starves because of some irreparable
failing in food processing systems.
The Adeptus Mechanicus is a religious order devoted to technology, and                            The Omnissiah's Faithful
exists as an almost autonomous segment of Imperial society. The ser-           The Techpriests of Mars are the hierachical rulers of the labyrinthine
vants of the Cult Mechanicus worship the Machine God they designate            order that is the Adeptus Mechanicus. They are all members of the Cult
as the Omnissiah, although many hold that this being is simply one facet       Mechanicus, the faith that worships the Machine-God or "Omnissiah".
of the Emperor Himself. The Adeptus Mechanicus is ruled from Mars,             The premiere engineers and technological specialists in the Imperium,
the oldest and greatest of humanity’s many forge worlds, where the             they accompany nearly every branch of the Imperium to maintain ma-
Fabricator General, the titular head of the order, resides. As one of the      chinery. Techpriests will provide religious rites, anti-corruption wards,
very first planets settled by Man upon exploring outwards from ancient         and repairs to machine-spirits to any machine they are qualified to over-
Terra, the red planet is strewn with the ruins of many thousands of years      see. Along with being technologically capable they are also, of course,
of industry, and incredible secrets are said to reside beneath its shifting,   priests in their order, giving counsel, admonishment, and other holy duty
rust-coloured deserts.                                                         to those among their flock.
           Aside from Mars, the Adeptus Mechanicus controls numerous                      Tech-Adept: Tech-adepts are the lowest rank of priesthood
forge worlds as an empire within the Imperium, manufactorums operat-           proper. They almost exclusively serve as acolytes to higher ranked tech-
ing on planetary scales. Each churns out an endless stream of technolog-       priests and magi, and may be sent on errands, missions, or as couriers for
ical goods to sustain the Imperium’s endless appetite for materiel, and        their masters. Tech-Adepts are notably less augmented than their superi-
in return receives a steady stream of raw materials, foodstuffs, and other     ors, and are often the most Human of those seen among the ranks of the
forms of payment. Though each forge world is—in theory at least—sub-           Mechanicus. They are, however, not very well-versed in the mysteries
servient to Mars, many guard their own secrets, and occasionally war           of the machine, but are often far more technologically adept than any
breaks out between rivals. Many hold a particular expertise in one or          Imperial citizen should be.
another technology, and most employ their own subtle (yet to the experi-                  Techpriest: Techpriests are often in anttendance of a magos,
enced eye, distinctive) variations of common designs, so it is possible to     one of the elders of the Mechanicus' order, though techpriests going
identify a plasma gun as the work of one specific world, for example, or       about their own business is far from unheard of. Not all techpriests have
a tank as the work of another.                                                 a singular vocation, and such individuals are sometimes called "Tech-

forge worlds’ industrial output, the Mechanicum sends its servants to                     Techpriest Secutor: Techpriest Secutors are found most

of all forms of technology. The common soldier might be entrusted to           commands. These techpriests are part war-savant and part frontline-com-
change the power pack in a standard-issue lasgun (so long as he recites        mander, applying their combat theory with pitiless efficiency, spending
the correct litanies), but he can scarcely be expected to repair a faulty      the lives of Skitarii to test their hypotheses and obtain valuable combat
field-cogitator. Thus, the Techpriests of the Cult Mechanicus are to be        metrics with every defeat or victory.
found wherever Mankind—and more importantly, Mankind’s technol-                           Techpriest Logis: The Logi are an order of techno-prophets
ogy—goes, conducting the rites of operation and repair over the war            within the Mechanicus, whose predicitions and prophesies guide much of
machines of the Imperium, assisted by legions of cybernetic servitors and      the Cult's action with a strong degree of prescience.
lesser menials.                                                                           Electropriest: The electropriests of the Cult Mechanicus

The soldiery of the Adeptus Mechanicus is the Legiones Skitarii. These                      Follower Type:                    Reinforcement Character Level:
cyborg soldiers are quite apart from the Taghmata of a forge world or the                   Lesser Machine                               Minoris
Titan Legions, with their ranks being made of the recruited faithful of a
                                                                                   Influence Cost:             Influence Minimum:           Other Requirements:
forge world's domain, rather than the God-Machines that are the Titans                    3                             30                   Peer (Mechanicus)
or the militant Techpriests of the Taghmata. These soldiers are under
the control of the Cult Mechanicus, but each legion has it's own com-           WS          BS        St       T         Ag         Int     Per      Wp         Fel
manders, in the form of a magi (often Secutors) and Masters of Skitari.         25          40       30        35        30         25      40           25        20
A Master of Skitarii is the leader of a cohort (roughly the equivalent of a          Movement: 3/6/9/18              Wounds: 9            Fatigue: 7
general being in charge of a regiment in the Imperial Guard), and beneath     Skills: Athletics +10, Awareness +10, Common Lore (Tech) +0, Disci-
him are Skitarius Marshals (who perform the same roles as officers), and      pline +0, Fortitude +0, Linguistics (Low Gothic, Techna Lingua) +0, Op-
then beneath Marshalls are Skitarius Alphas, the squad leaders of Skitarii.   erate (Aeronautica, Surface) +0, Scrutiny +10, Survival +0, Tech-Use +0
           The process of conversion from a simple adherent of the            Talents: Autosanguine Implants, Chem-Geld, Combat Sense, Deadeye
Omnissiac faith to a soldier of the Machine-God is neither universal nor      Shot, Endurance (2)
publicly disclosed. Every forge world has its own variant on the process,     Traits: Skitarius Implants
as some recruit base don the strength of a subject's faith, others simply     Cybernetics: Bionic Legs, Bionic Respiratory System, Good Cybernetic
mass-recruiting workers from manufactora, and others still having an en-      Senses (Sight), Vox Implant
tire class of subject raised and bred for the purpose. From there, through    Gear: Salvation Augur, Good Vox Caster, Vid-Relay
the implantation of various bionics, psycho indoctrination (including the     Armour: Light Carapace Chestpiece & Helm (AP5, Head, Body)
uploading of weapon-training engrams, doctrina activation programs,           Weapons: Good Lasrifle
and obedience protocols), and various other additions, skitarii are forged.
This process too varies from forge world to forge world, with the ulti-                                             Good Lasrifle
mate product varying greatly. Skitarii from some forge worlds are more           Class           Basic         Range          100m        Rate of Fire        S/-/-
akin to servitors capable of independant thought, while others are little       Damage                       1d10+5E                      Penetration          0
more than guardsmen with additional augmetics, but no few forge worlds
                                                                                  Clip                          35                          Reload            Full
utilize Mars Alpha-patterns in their armies (with Rangers, Vanguards,
and Sicarians holding particular prominence over those close to Mars).          Qualities                           Accurate, Variable, Reliable

                                                                                                    Kastelan Battle-Automata: One of the more common extant
                                                                                        variants of true automata, Kastelan are still somewhat unsophisticated
                                                                                        compared to many others. This simplicity, however, has allowed for them
                                                                                        to remain in use for many millennia, whereas most others have become
                                                                                        wonders of archeotech, with little knowledge of their systems existing.
                                                                                                    Castellax Battle-Automata: Once the workhorse and main-
                                                                                        stay of the Legio Cybernetica, the Castellax has fallen by the wayside for
                                                                                        numerous reasons. All the same, these great machines are powerful and
                                                                                        destructive in equal measure, being capable of taking out enemy armour
                                                                                        as well as striding into melee against enemy troops with near impunity.
                                                                                                    Vorax Hunter-Killer Automata: Bearing something of a
                                                                                        sinister reputation, Vorax are most well-known for executing culls of
                                                                                        rebelling members of forge worlds populace, or else cleansing the lower
                                                                                        levels of forge-fanes from mutants and other dregs. When turned to the
                                                                                        field of battle, their skill set is applied to the culling of enemy infantry, a
                                                                                        role it excels at.

               Mechanicus Vehicles                                              pressures. Along with the flexibility of its wings, the Archaeopter is able
                                                                                to assume the role of bomber, ground attack vehicle, or combat transport
While the Mechanicus is the main source of vehicles and other technol-
                                                                                with ease, and is often launched from orbit by Mechanicus forces to
ogy for the Imperium of Man, there are certain, revered patterns of vehi-
                                                                                pursue goals within a planet's atmosphere.
cles that are held in reserve for forces of the Mechanicus only. Some of
these vehicles are kept due to their nature as powerful archeotech, while
others are merely kept out of tradition and an appreciation for the pattern.                                 Ironstrider
The Mechanicus, of course, still makes use of vehicles such as Rhinos,            Hull Integrity:           18             Armour:                14/14/12
Predators, Land Raiders, and Baneblades, but the vehicles presented here
are those of this special variety that they set aside for their own use.         Tactical Speed:           12m
                                                                                                                          Maneuver:                   +20

engine, these tall, lithe machines are possessed of an unresting ma-                   Type:                                 Walker
chine-spirit. They are swift and capable of crossing most kind of terrain,                                                                        Very Rare/
with the Legiones Skitarii using them for scouting and hunter-killer                   Size:                 6           Availability:
                                                                                                                                                   Ŧ1,750
missions.

are not particularly swift, but they can bring weapons of great power to
bear, and when in a squadron the unique functions of each vehicle's force              Crew:                                   Pilot
field amplify, protecting them from a disproportionate volume of enemy               Weapon             Pilot-Operated Forward-Facing Hull-Mounted
fire.                                                                                Mounts:                Twin Autocannon -or- Twin Lascannon

                                                                                                                  Special:
of the Skorpius engine the Disintegrator mounts powerful weapons that
allow the swift tank to encircle enemies, bombard them, and bring about            Perpetual Motion Engine: Due to its unique propulsion system, an
their destruction before they can be maneuvered against.                            Ironstrider is unable to willingly stop moving. In game terms this

to providing greater protection for its passengers, the Termite's tunneling

foes.

                                                                 12.8 Adeptus Mechanicus - 973
                                        12.9 Adeptus Ministorum
While he walked among His people, the Emperor denied His own divinity, yet His miraculous reincarnation upon the Golden Throne served to pro-
ve His godhood to the bereaved masses. In the aftermath of the Horus Heresy, the Emperor came to be worshipped by a staggering array of different
groups, often possessed of highly divergent ideas regarding the Emperor’s life, death, and continued existence. It was perhaps inevitable that
conflict should ensue between these divergent sects, but within a thousand years of the Emperor’s ascension, one had risen above them all. The
Ecclesiarchy proved itself the strongest faction by a process of absorbing all other groups it came into contact with, adapting doctrines and forging
links in order to make it appear they had been there all along. It was eventually recognised as the one, true faith of the Imperium, its status confir-
med with the title Adeptus Ministorum; other sects that refused to bow down to its rule were declared heretics and marked for destruction.
In the 41st Millennium, the Ecclesiarchy holds sway over the hearts and
minds of all but a few of the Emperor’s subjects. The Adeptus Minis-

                                                                                           Preacher: A preacher is the leader of a shrine at the centre of
                                                                               a parish, which is part of a diocese, of which there are thousands. They
                                                                               serve the local population and are stern and unforgiving, seeing it as their
                                                                               duty to hound out heresy among their flock. They often preside over the
                                                                               public execution of discovered psykers, mutants and other deviants.
                                                                               In the Imperial Guard Preachers are represented by Ministorum Priests
                                                                               and led their brethren in battle, inspiring them with their passionate
                                                                               oratory even under attack from the enemy. Other Preachers accompany
                                                                               Inquisitors in their quests against Chaos.

                     Castigator Battle Tank                                                                Exorcist Artillery Tank
Castigators are powerful battle tanks of the Adepta Sororitas. They pro-              The Exorcist is a weapon uniquely representative of the ways in which
vide excellent fire support for Battle Sisters thanks to their wide array of          the Adepta Sororitas pair shows of devotion to the Emperor with the cas-
weapons, smashing through the foe's lines and slaughtering great swathes              tigation of his heretical enemies. It is a rolling shrine to the magnificence
of enemy infantry in a matter of seconds. Like the Predator of the Space              of the Master of Mankind, a breathtakingly ornate piece of craftsmanship
Marines, the Castigator is built on a Rhino chassis. Similar to its Predator          that grinds into battle projecting inspirational hymnals to the Emperor’s
cousin, it fulfills a dedicated fire support role on the battlefield.                 glory. These carry across the field of battle and swell all good Imperial
       Hull:                  45             Armour:                 42/31/20         hearts with faith and pride. When entire squadrons of these vehicles
                                                                                      advance in armoured spearheads, their combined symphonic emanations
 Tactical Speed:             15m                                                      transform even the most mud-and-blood-drenched field of war into a

                                                                 12.9 Adeptus Ministorum - 980
                     Immolator Battle Tank
Used exclusively by the Adeptus Ministorum, the Immolator-pattern
armoured assault transport is a variant on the Rhino chassis. Atop the hull
of each Immolator is mounted an elaborate turret arrangement that incor-
porates a stained-armaglass ballistic shield, auto-choral targeting array
and gunner's nest linked to a twinned pair of devastating heavy weapons.

                                                               12.9 Adeptus Ministorum - 981
                                             12.10 Imperial Guard
Each planet in the Imperium must look first to its own defence against the numerous foes that assail the Emperor’s domains, and to this end almost
all maintain some form of local defence force, whether standing army or irregular militia. When war comes and the world in question cannot
contain the enemy on its own, it broadcasts the call for aid, a call that is answered by the raising of Astra Militarum regiments from amongst the
defence forces of neighbouring worlds. In addition to these emergency situations, the worlds of the Imperium are all subject to a regular tithe, often
a rendering of manpower to serve in the ongoing, relentless wars of the Imperium, in the multitudinous ranks of the Imperial Guard.
Drawn as they are from a staggering array of worlds and cultures, some
well used to technology and others living in primitive squalor, no two                            Imperial Guardsman (Troop)
regiments of the Imperial Guard are exactly alike. Even its signature                       Follower Type:                      Reinforcement Character Level:
weapon, the ubiquitous, mass-produced lasgun, is subject to variation                       Lesser Follower                                Minoris
depending on the manufactorum, munitorum pattern, and local forge                  Influence Cost:             Influence Minimum:            Other Requirements:
traditions that guided their creation.                                                    2                             30                   Peer (Imperial Guard)
           Regardless of the specifics, the Imperial Guard represents the        WS         BS       St        T         Ag        Int       Per      Wp        Fel
hammer that crushes the enemies of the Emperor. Its ranks are unending,
                                                                                 35         35       35        35        30          25      35          30         30
for Mankind is blessed in manpower above all other resources. Each and
every day, untold thousands of Guardsmen give their lives in the service             Movement: 3/6/9/18              Wounds: 9           Fatigue: 6
of the Emperor, a pious battle cry upon their lips and a devoted prayer        Skills: Athletics +10, Awareness +0, Common Lore (War) +0, Discipline
within their hearts.                                                           +0, Dodge +0, Fortitude +0, Operate (Surface) +10, Survival +0
           Imperial Guardsman: The ubiquitous Imperial Guardsman               Talents: Weapon Drill (Las)
can be any Imperial citizen, man or woman, and when the Imperium has           Traits: Disciplined (Horde)
great need the forces of the Guard will have mass recruitments where           Gear: Lho Sticks, The Infantryman’s Uplifting Primer, Universal Stan-
planets are stripped of thousands or even millions of its people at a time.    dard Kit
Many of these soldiers do not live past their first day when they see com-     Armour: Flak Armour (AP4, All) (Main Armour)
bat against the enemies of Humanity.                                           Weapons: Lasgun (Main Weapon), Knife, Frag Grenade, Krak Grenade
           Veteran Guardsman: Those who have seen the enemies of                                                       Lasgun
Man and fought them face to face are hardened and jaded individu-
                                                                                  Class          Basic         Range          100m        Rate of Fire        S/2/3
als, and are likely to have developed a hatred for particular heretics or
xenos, and attendant skills that allow them to kill such enemies with            Damage                       1d10+3E                     Penetration          0
single-minded violence. The veteran guardsman is a greatly competant              Clip                          60                           Reload           Full
fighter, soldier, and hunter with hard-won experience, and often times the      Qualities                               Reliable, Variable
scars to back it up.
           Imperial Guard Officer: Officers are stern and uncompro-                                                     Knife
mising, they enforce the orders of the regiment's general and command             Class          Melee         Range          Melee       Rate of Fire        -/-/-
the vast ranks of men at the front of platoons and companies. Many of
                                                                                 Damage                   1d5R+3StB                       Penetration          0
the lower ranks in the Guard have what ranges from grudging respect to
veiled contempt for these men, as they are often a class apart from the         Qualities                                     Piercing
common soldiery.
                                                                                                                    Frag Grenade
           Imperial Guard General: Generals of the Imperial Guard
command entire regiments of guardsmen, and execute battleplans that               Class          Thrown        Range            9m        Rate of Fire        -/-/-
utilize such numbers in pitched battle. Such responsibility and power cre-       Damage                       2d10X                       Penetration          0
ates men of iron will, though their particular presence upon the battlefield      Clip                           1                           Reload             -
itself varies by disposition. Some are roaring warriors who lead from the
front, while some others prefer to gaze over hololithic intelligence maps       Qualities                                     Blast (3)
and relay orders through officers and vox-comms. Regardless of mien
                                                                                                                   Krak Grenade
these Imperial servants can save entire worlds with their presence, and
attendant armies and materiel.                                                    Class          Thrown        Range            9m        Rate of Fire        -/-/-
           Storm Trooper: Storm troopers are trained to carry out special        Damage                       2d10+4X                     Penetration          6
operations such as deep strike assaults, reconnaissance and infiltration          Clip                           1                           Reload             -
beyond enemy lines. Storm troopers are the very best soldiers the Guard
                                                                                Qualities                          Concussive (0), Vengeful (9)
can call upon, being trained to the peak of human perfection, their com-
bat skills are unmatched by any normal guardsman. The rigid discipline
of Ordo Tempestus training allows them to carry out any order regardless
of how inhumane it may be. Fully prepared to give their own lives for the
Imperium, storm troopers are indoctrinated with a sense of obedience and
duty that overrides any instinct of personal safety.
           Commissar: Commissars are special Imperial officers assigned
to Imperial Guard regiments and Imperial Navy ships, whose purpose is
to enforce discipline and devotion to the Emperor of Mankind. Commis-
sars are invariably stern and uncompromising individuals, able to keep
even the most anarchic regiments in line through fear, strength and pow-
er. They have authority over both the soldiers and regimental officers,
with the right and duty to immediately execute any guardsman or officer
who shows cowardice or incompetence in battle.

                                                                          12.10 Imperial Guard - 984
                                              12.11 Imperial Navy
Every vessel bound to the service of Mankind is ultimately subject to the control and regulation of the Imperial Fleet, its masters registered with
the segmentum fortresses. The greatest part is the Imperial Navy, the body responsible for fighting the enemies of the Emperor in the depths of the
airless void and supporting His armies on the ground.

It falls to the warships of the Imperial Navy to conduct a wide range of
missions. Many spend long months, even years, on extended patrols of                           Imperial Navy Rating (Troop)
Imperial space, their presence deterring pirates and alien raiders. Some                  Follower Type:                     Reinforcement Character Level:
squadrons are engaged in continuous operations against known concen-                       Lesser Human                                 Minoris
trations of slavers, and fight ongoing campaigns to keep the Warp routes         Influence Cost:           Influence Minimum:              Other Requirements:
clear and commerce flowing. Occasionally, warships gather into massive                  2                           30                     Peer (Imperial Navy)
fleets in order to prosecute wars against alien empires or defend crucial      WS         BS       St       T         Ag           Int     Per      Wp          Fel
systems against invasion. These confrontations see the warships of both
                                                                               35         35       35       35        30           25      35           30         30
sides unleash weapons of unimaginable destructive potential, slaying
thousands of enemies with each fusillade. The largest of the Imperial              Movement: 3/6/9/18               Wounds: 9          Fatigue: 6
Navy’s warships are host to hundreds of thousands of crew, for machines      Skills: Acrobatics +0, Athletics +10, Awareness +0, Common Lore (War)
are venerated relics of history often impossible to replace, while men are   +0, Discipline +0, Dodge +0, Fortitude +0, Operate (Aeronautica) +10,
many and without limit.                                                      Trade (Technomat) +0

has need for them. Ratings fill every conceivable position that does not     Gear: Universal Standard Kit
require expertise or experience aboard the ship, they are technomatic        Armour: Flak Armour (AP4, All) with Enviro-Sealed Modification
repairmen, boarding parties, gunnery crews, and small craft pilots.          Weapons: Autogun -or- Automatic Shotgun, Knife, Frag Grenade

have earned some merits in the eyes of the ship's officers and can gain
                                                                                Class          Basic       Range           100m          Rate of Fire        S/3/10
a position such as a gunnery sergeant, deck-bosun, and sometimes even

             Imperial Navy Captain: Each captain of the Imperial Navy is
                                                                              Qualities                                    Blast (3)
the lord and master of a small kingdom in the void. Under their lead-
ership fusillades of fire that could end a planet and destroy a hive city
are directed. As such these men are seen as the height of naval authority
(aside from Admirals and Lord Admirals, that is) and should embody
proper conduct as dictated by the Navy's culture. Most follow such
guidelines, being stentorian and authoritative, with those who do not
meet such standards being sent far afield, on scouting missions or remote
patrols.
